# Plan 179 browser test consolidation

Oracle locations reflect the final Plan 180 independent-axis rows.

This is the historical Plan 179 report. Plan 180 supersedes its ownership-suite boundary and matrix registration audit. Current assertion destinations below have been retargeted; historical counts, outcomes and hashes remain observations of the original run. See [Plan 180](180-ownership-suite-reduction.md) for current source totals and direct adapter unit-test acceptance.

Baseline B is commit `19026afe0fef6e934f9e02d8b19af30168021fd6` on 2026-10-28. A source marked `B:apps/...:line` identifies that immutable Git version, not a path in the consolidated tree.

## Verification and measurements

Bun is 1.4.3 with `$HOME/.bun/bin` prepended to PATH. Every browser command takes `scripts/with-flow-browser-lock.sh` separately and uses one worker. Playwright owns server startup and cleanup. No process was stopped by name or port.

The baseline commands are `bunx playwright test --workers=1 --reporter=line` for Vanilla, and the same command with `-c apps/vue/playwright.config.ts` or `-c apps/foldkit/playwright.config.ts`. Each command runs through the browser lock with `caffeinate -di`.

These are single complete-suite observations, not a repeatable speedup claim. The wall times below are Playwright suite durations. Outer shell durations include queueing for the shared lock and cannot measure suite cost. Vanilla's unqueued outer wall time was 652.56 seconds. The machine has 16 logical CPUs; baseline load averages were 7.55, 3.93 and 3.68. Concurrent branches use the same lock.

| Host | Before discovered tests | Before results | Before spec lines | Before wall time | After discovered tests | After spec lines | After wall time |
| --- | --: | --- | --: | --- | --: | --: | --- |
| vanilla | 1111 | 1107 passed, 4 skipped | 33736 | 10.9 min | 1064 | 32140 | 10.3 min |
| vue | 1240 | 1238 passed, 2 skipped | 22422 | 10.5 min | 1002 | 20695 | 9.0 min |
| foldkit | 842 | 842 passed, 0 skipped | 20361 | 10.4 min | 842 | 18877 | 10.2 min |

The spec-line metric counts each host's `.e2e.ts` files. Helpers and shared contracts are reported separately so moving assertions does not appear as deletion. A focused run does not establish full-suite acceptance. Final acceptance is the locked `bun run verify --full` pass: profile `full`, `fullSuite: true`, exit code 0, completed at 12:39:54 EDT on 2026-10-28 in 1,808,886 ms (30 minutes 8.9 seconds). The three after measurements are the complete host suites inside that verifier command, each using its normal host configuration and one worker.

After results: Vanilla 1,062 passed and 2 skipped; Vue 1,000 passed and 2 skipped; Foldkit 842 passed and none skipped. Before and after counts include discovered skips. Expected-failure classifications in the upstream ports are unchanged.

The focused runs were Vanilla 278/280, Vue 296/296, and Foldkit 89/91. The failures exposed test-consolidation errors: the reconnect-anchor class describes control activation, the chooser selectors needed to exclude minimap ghosts, Foldkit connections require initialized measured handles, and the public rectangle-selection event includes its selected edges. The kept styling assertion, original screenshot input state, geometry readiness, and literal selection event were corrected. A locked retry passed both Foldkit cases. A temporary multi-project Vanilla retry changed snapshot suffixes and timed out after returning to the main playground; the same tests passed 2/2 in 4.6 seconds through the normal Vanilla configuration without further source changes.

The first full attempt stopped at the isolated Foldkit app build because the shared setup referenced a Vue-only window declaration. Host setup and geometry initialization now live in typed host callbacks. The isolated Foldkit typecheck and the final complete full verifier pass. All 391 host browser source/snapshot and shared test files remained byte-identical throughout that successful full run. Final follow-up edits only retarget evidence records, record these results, and strengthen the matrix-audit inventory guard; they do not change browser assertions or runtime code.

Final evidence follow-up checks pass: quality, documentation, catalog coverage, API member inventory, contract ownership, proof matrix, example recipes, upstream ledger, research citations, the 21-family matrix audit, and its direct TypeScript check. The metadata audit preserves 29,287 historical status/count fields and all recorded source hashes.

## Plan decisions

1. Threshold admission stays in `node-threshold.e2e.ts`, including `nodeThresholdContract` for all three hosts. Native capture ordering remains host-specific. Renderer faults, viewport ownership, and other gesture callback cases now have behavior-named suites and matching helpers. Their assertions remain intact. Pure session detachment, ordered disposal, pending-threshold release, other-pointer isolation, repeat terminals, and same-pointer reentry are unit-tested in `packages/core/src/domain/flow-interaction-order.test.ts`.
2. Intersection and edge-geometry recipes each use one shared contract with explicit host selectors. Schema Studio shares app behavior while each host keeps renderer-specific layering, context-menu, minimap, inline-edit, reconnect, and width-resize cases. Vue and Foldkit run the same lifecycle contract for connection normalization, full/partial selection, and input flags. Their native callback, keyboard, cancellation, resize, and Runtime-specific cases remain per host.
3. Delivery-phase filenames now describe behavior. Unique assertions remain in the renamed suites. Proven overlaps are removed: Vanilla partial selection and selection auto-pan use the kept lifecycle and input-configuration cases; Foldkit resize acceptance uses the cancellation/retry case's identical accepted width. Canvas UI public contracts and panel tests share one Vanilla file. Repeated control-visibility checks use the kept actionable-control test. The large duplicate canvas demo check is replaced by the chooser smoke and the dedicated feature tests.
4. The two throwing `test.fixme` placeholders are deleted. Fixed `waitForTimeout` calls outside upstream-ledger ports use counted browser frame windows, or existing polling assertions. Selection checks use public `aria-pressed` values in Vanilla and public change events in Foldkit. The selected minimap and reconnect-anchor classes remain styling assertions. Each demo entry file has one smoke; unique viewport-scene wiring remains in viewport-operations.

The original threshold and reentry matrix axes retain representative values. `representativeCombination` keeps the baseline tuple and varies one axis at a time. Every value of every axis is represented without the full Cartesian product. Rows for native capture ordering and native successor scenarios retain their original expansion. Higher-order combinations that differ only in axis cross-products are intentionally omitted. This reduces browser work without weakening the retained literal assertions.

The large Vue viewport expected-value tables now use literal event and viewport constructors. A separate evaluation of all three before/after oracle objects produced deeply equal values. No expected value is calculated from the implementation or its observed output.

The four named threshold/reentry files go from 20,484 to 1,201 lines, a 94.1% reduction. This named-file target includes rehoming non-threshold behavior. It does not mean 70% of browser source was deleted. The host totals above include the rehomed suites.

- vanilla browser specs plus helpers: 38,868 to 37,322 lines.
- vue browser specs plus helpers: 27,549 to 25,857 lines.
- foldkit browser specs plus helpers: 27,403 to 25,933 lines.

Shared browser TypeScript, including retained upstream ports: 11,666 to 13,485 lines. This source is counted once, not once per host.

No upstream-ledger port changes. `research/artifacts/` remains immutable. `verification.config.json` changes only the renamed filenames and removes the merged Vanilla canvas-UI-contract entry. Plans 177 and 178 own the verification model and existing unit-test pruning.

The catalog, proof matrix, member contract and example-recipe references are audited. Renamed file references and review-round titles use the current entries. The proof matrix and member contracts that already reference the retained lifecycle files or shared contract need no path change. Archived result outcomes remain historical observations; retargeting their source locations and titles does not create a new parity result. Historical source-hash values also remain unchanged; renamed hash keys are aliases to prior-run locations, not hashes of the consolidated source.

## Production and test boundaries

Production Vanilla enters through `apps/vanilla/src/public.ts:9` and `mountFlow` at `apps/vanilla/src/mount-flow.ts:15129`. The rendered node's native listener is at `apps/vanilla/src/mount-flow.ts:9790`, admission calls `nodeDragActivated` at `apps/vanilla/src/mount-flow.ts:9857`, constrained drag geometry is owned at `apps/vanilla/src/mount-flow.ts:6979`, accepted semantic publication handles node positions at `apps/vanilla/src/mount-flow.ts:8704`, and node/viewport transforms reach the DOM at `apps/vanilla/src/mount-flow.ts:1795`.

Production Vue exports `FlowCanvas` at `packages/vue/src/public.ts:1`. Its vnode forwards native input at `packages/vue/src/flow-canvas.ts:9991` to the handler at `packages/vue/src/flow-canvas.ts:7185`. Movement checks the threshold at `packages/vue/src/flow-canvas.ts:7037`, builds position changes at `packages/vue/src/flow-canvas.ts:6861`, and renders accepted/preview coordinates at `packages/vue/src/flow-canvas.ts:9917`. Component disposal owns resource release at `packages/vue/src/flow-canvas.ts:11250`.

Production Foldkit exports `createProgram` at `packages/foldkit/src/public.ts:2`. `packages/foldkit/src/application.ts:4382` composes the pointer resource. The rendered canvas binds native pointerdown at `packages/foldkit/src/graph.ts:8323`, which enters the capture/admission resource at `packages/foldkit/src/graph.ts:7244`. The graph checks threshold admission at `packages/foldkit/src/graph.ts:4202`, release reduces through `packages/foldkit/src/graph.ts:2429`, and rendered absolute positions become node styles at `packages/foldkit/src/graph.ts:7804`.

The shared browser threshold path starts at `apps/shared-e2e/node-threshold-contract.ts:118`. It mounts each actual host through the connection-threshold fixture, uses native mouse and keyboard APIs, and reads public changes, accepted positions, capture state, and rendered bounds. Its geometric threshold is the core strict-distance predicate at `packages/core/src/domain/flow-node-drag-threshold.schema.ts:18`. This is distinct from synthetic reentry fixtures, which replace capture methods to exercise callback ordering. Synthetic receipts are not proof of trusted native capture. The retained native host cases supply that boundary.

The matrix selection audit is rerunnable with `bun run research/traces/179-browser-matrix-check.ts`. It enumerates every value of each declared axis in the retained registration code and checks the representative selection covers those values. It also requires the reviewed inventory of 21 matrix families. This checks registration selection; the baseline assertion map supplies the original source identities.

The shared Foldkit connection cases start the real mounted measurement observer and poll `nodesInitialized` before native input. This matches the existing named-handle proof and avoids treating mounted markup as initialized geometry.

The new package unit tests call the core session API directly. They prove immutable session reductions and cleanup order, not browser event propagation or framework scheduling. The browser suites retain those latter assertions. Schema Studio enters the actual Vanilla app at `apps/vanilla/schema-studio/main.ts:882` and the Foldkit Runtime at `apps/foldkit/schema-studio/main.ts:182`; shared app model assertions do not replace renderer-specific proof.

Foldkit selection tests read actual public change publications through `apps/foldkit/e2e/upstream-changes.ts:6`. The fixture subscribes to the Runtime changes port, decodes once, records the transient event, and applies the existing acceptance path at `apps/foldkit/src/upstream-fixture.ts:209`. No event log enters saved graph state, history, or collaboration. Native selection checks remain at `apps/foldkit/e2e/node-input.e2e.ts:16`, `apps/foldkit/e2e/edge-interaction.e2e.ts:5`, and `apps/foldkit/e2e/selection-rect-race.e2e.ts:7`.

## Assertion map

Each row identifies one distinct baseline assertion by source line and SHA-256 prefix. A literal deep equality assertion includes its complete nested expected object. The description is abbreviated only for display. The target is the kept assertion, or the entry of the kept test/contract when the assertion is consolidated. Repeated parameter combinations share the same source assertion row.

The explicit drops are limited to fixture-array contents, repeated demo copy/decorations, redundant scene remount counts, and CSS-class proxies whose behavior is checked by a public event or subsequent native movement. Arbitrary fixture coordinates are replaced by the named dedicated geometry/viewport tests, not by a parity claim.

Decision codes identify the exact retention or removal reason. D codes are intentional drops.

- K01: Kept with the same test behavior.
- K02: Shared assertion; each host supplies its production selector and runs this test.
- K03: The same native hit-target test now asserts the public edge-selection change with its literal edge id.
- K04: Common behavior is asserted in the named kept test or shared contract.
- K05: The same native test asserts the actual public selection-set change event instead of a selected class.
- K06: Shared app assertion; host selectors and scheduler barriers preserve the rendered-host boundary.
- K07: One literal public selection-set event lists all three node ids, replacing three CSS-class proxies after the same native rectangle.
- K08: The same kept test proves selection through public keyboard movement and one-step undo. Drop the CSS-class proxy.
- K09: The kept public-controls test clicks these controls and checks their viewport effects. The composition screenshot also remains.
- K10: Kept with the same test behavior. Selection state now uses the host public aria-pressed value; decoration-only checks remain where styling is the subject.
- D11: Intentionally drop repeated demo copy and active-button decoration checks. The chooser smoke retains graph switching, visibility actions and snapshots.
- K12: Dedicated culling and hidden-node tests retain visible-set and incident-edge removal checks; the smoke retains scene switching.
- K13: The shared contract compares all five rendered path kinds with public geometry, including cubic curves.
- K14: Native selection and arrow movement remain in node-movement and canvas keyboard tests; the arbitrary demo transform literal is redundant. Selection state now uses the host public aria-pressed value; decoration-only checks remain where styling is the subject.
- K15: Native selection and arrow movement remain in node-movement and canvas keyboard tests; the arbitrary demo transform literal is redundant.
- D16: Intentionally drop the fixture-array identity check. It tests checked-in data, not a browser. The kept loop still drives every current connection case.
- K17: Common behavior is asserted in the named kept test or shared contract. Selection state now uses the host public aria-pressed value; decoration-only checks remain where styling is the subject.
- K18: Kept in the behavior suite. Repeated Cartesian combinations use independent axis coverage; native input stays per host.
- K19: Native parent resize, eight controls, proposal and unchanged child geometry remain in the dedicated nested-resize tests.
- K20: The shared native drag-handle contract checks ordinary-control exclusion and a successful handle drag in this host.
- D21: Intentionally drop repeated scene-remount decoration counts. The smoke still switches all scenes, checks a single canvas and verifies disposal; canvas UI tests retain exact panel cleanup.
- K22: Viewport transforms and fit are covered by dedicated native viewport tests. Drop the demo-specific minus-25 pixel literal; retain scene wiring in one smoke.
- K23: Kept locator, readiness or scheduler assertion in the shared or renamed helper.

The removed Vanilla placeholders at baseline lines 914 and 918 contained no behavior assertions. Their only statement threw a request for real pointer capture. Native multi-input and capture coverage remains in the shared threshold contracts and host lifecycle suites; removing placeholders does not claim device-level two-contact capture parity.

## Conformity and deviations

- Conforming: every kept host still executes native pointer and keyboard coverage, plus the shared threshold contract. Recipe contracts assert rendered output through explicit host selectors. The assertion map preserves unique browser observations when tests move or share code.
- Intentionally different: independent axis coverage replaces Cartesian expansion to reduce repeated browser setup. Higher-order interactions outside the selected rows remain outside this acceptance boundary. Pure session ordering runs as package unit tests; actual capture, DOM propagation, callback reentry and framework disposal remain browser responsibilities.
- Intentionally different: Vanilla exposes node/edge selection as `aria-pressed`, so those assertions use that existing public state instead of inventing `aria-selected`. Foldkit selection tests read real public change events. Minimap and reconnect-anchor selected-class checks remain because class rendering and control activation are their subjects.
- Intentionally different: named threshold-file line reduction includes moving unrelated renderer and viewport tests into behavior-named files. Whole-host and shared-source totals expose that accounting boundary.
- Unverified: no new upstream parity, screen-reader output, collector delivery, or physical multi-touch capture claim follows from this consolidation. The full verifier result and the host suite counts delimit local acceptance.
